Then either confirm a Teams end on evidence available within seconds (mic released, Leave gone, and one discovery-style scoring pass inside the hold showing no call controls) and rely on the existing 120 s resume to stitch a mic-switch drop back together (the one seen here lasted 22 s), or keep the 60 s but stop presenting it as a live recording and apply the trim without asking.

**D104. A Leave button that reads healthy again after the mic was released reverts the hold, and nothing re-opens it**

- Severity medium (confirmed). Location `daemon/Sources/MeetingPipeCore/Lifecycle/Signals/AXLeaveButtonSignal.swift:414`. Related: D041, D045, G025, F033, F031. Effort M.
- Owner sees: Zoom end missed: after leaving, the HUD drops Confirming end and goes back to plain recording
- Mechanism: At HEAD (and in the work laptop's newest build era), when the Leave button's cached element reads healthy again (no re-walk) during a hold, PromotionEngine.handleEndingProvisional reverts to inMeeting because the reading is in the lead's own evidence class. It does this even though process audio and window-gone in `states` have already ended. After that revert nothing is pending. tick() only acts in endingProvisional. ProcessAudioSignal and ShareableContentSignal emit on change only. SilenceAutoStopPolicy stands down for a native source while the lifecycle is live. So the recording keeps going until one of five things happens: the Leave probe flips to invalid again, Zoom quits, a low-space or backlog stop fires, or the user presses Stop. The revert itself is demonstrated once, at 2026-11-12T15:19:53.344Z for us.zoom.xos in the 20261112-170131 recording, newest era. The unbounded run is only code-derived, because the user's Stop came at 15:19:54.638Z. At c56f2869 this shape cannot occur: there, the cross-class process-audio ended at 15:19:49 would have confirmed the end at once through the END6 fast path.
- Field: demonstrated. signal ax_leave_button_state with previous invalid and state healthy, no ax_leave_button_rearmed in the preceding 100 ms, while the bundle's last process_audio_is_running_input is false; then lifecycle in_meeting and lifecycle_end_hold_released. Found once: 2026-11-12T15:19:53.344Z. To prove the unbounded run, look for the same shape followed by auto_stop_silence_skipped with no stop; not found, because the manual stop came at 15:19:54.638Z.
- Verifier: Code. The snippet exists verbatim at AXLeaveButtonSignal.swift:409-415 at HEAD. It maps every AX status except invalidUIElement to healthy. In _evaluate, a healthy read whose previous state was invalid calls onChange(healthy). This needs no rearmed or self_armed event when the same cached element simply stops returning invalidUIElement. PromotionEngine.handleEndingProvisional (HEAD) handles that .live and reverts to inMeeting when `event.kind.evidenceClass == leading.evidenceClass || returning || vetoesLoneEnd`. An axLeaveButton lead receiving axLeaveButton .live satisfies both of the first two conditions. The revert ignores that processAudioIsRunningInput and shareableContentWindow are recorded as ended in `states`. In inMeeting, tick() is a no-op. ProcessAudioSignal.evaluate returns .steady when lastValue == value. ShareableContentSignal emits only when state.lastValue != present. Neither signal re-emits ended, so no new end edge arrives unless the Leave probe flips to invalid again. If it did, the hold would re-open, because process audio is ended and there is no veto. handleIdleAutoStop checks SilenceAutoStopPolicy.shouldAutoStop, which returns false for a native source with a live lifecycle, and logs auto_stop_silence_skipped. I found no max-duration cap; the only cap is the writer backlog cap. So the claimed mechanism holds and I found no guard that refutes it.

Field pack. I ran the session scratchpad and cnt.py over all four events*.jsonl files, sorted by ts. The sequence for us.zoom.xos pid 17242 matches the claim exactly:
- 15:19:45.186Z: ax_leave_button_state healthy to invalid, then lifecycle ending_provisional (leading ax_leave_button_invalid) and lifecycle_end_held (live_signal call_is_up).
- 15:19:49.322Z: process_audio_is_running_input true to false.
- 15:19:50.967Z: shareable_content_window_present true to false.
- 15:19:53.344Z: ax_leave_button_state invalid to healthy (reason health_poll), with no rearmed or self_armed event nearby.
- 15:19:53.350Z: lifecycle in_meeting. 15:19:53.356Z: lifecycle_end_hold_released (held_sec 8.17).
- 15:19:54.638Z: state_change recording to stopping, with no lifecycle ended before it.

Count definitions: an invalid-to-healthy transition is any ax_leave_button_state event with previous invalid and state healthy. The signature additionally requires no rearmed or self_armed event within 100 ms before it, and the same bundle's last process_audio_is_running_input value to be false. My counts: 30 invalid-to-healthy transitions pack-wide, and exactly 1 matching the signature (the 15:19:53.344Z event above). This is in the newest build era (after the 2026-11-12 09:19Z rebuild). Neither file has changed since that rebuild, so the era code matches HEAD.

c56f2869. There, handleEndingProvisional confirms at once on any cross-class ended. The process-audio drop at 15:19:49 would have ended the meeting before the Leave revert, so this defect is head_only.

Severity lowered to medium. It was seen once in about 113 meetings, and the user's own Stop came 1.3 s later. The unbounded recording is inferred rather than observed, although nothing in the code would bound it before the user stops.
- Direction: A same-class live reading should not revert a hold once the vetoing class has read ended; at most it restarts the dwell. Make the probe three-valued (invalid, healthy only on a successful read, unknown otherwise) and keep the engine's tick armed after any revert that leaves an ended class behind.

**D105. Older builds: a failed Leave re-arm at the end left the Leave signal with no poll, so a mic release was never corroborated**

- Severity medium (confirmed). Location `daemon/Sources/MeetingPipeCore/Lifecycle/Signals/AXLeaveButtonSignal.swift:150`. Related: D047 (both recordings were attached starts, but that is not the cause), END8, PERF15. Effort S.
- Owner sees: The Teams call ended and the recording carried on until the idle backstop or a manual stop
- Mechanism: Before PERF15 (200a4971, 2026-10-26), AXLeaveButtonSignal._start called _stop(), set context, element and resolver, then threw from axBus.subscribe before startPoll and the initial _evaluate; _armIfNeeded caught it and logged ax_leave_button_arm_failed. The element was actually kept, but with no poll and no destroyed subscription nothing ever re-read it, so Leave could not go invalid. The file was unchanged from 2026-06-25 to PERF15, so c56f2869 (home Mac) and both work-laptop eras A and B had it; HEAD's subscribeDestroyed logs and still starts the poll. Field, era A: of 3 arm failures, 2026-10-06T14:34:37.313Z and 2026-10-08T13:40:24.882Z each came about 0.2 to 1.0 s after a provisional end led by process_audio false and a provisional_end_rescued, with no ax_leave_button_state and no lifecycle ended afterwards; the first ran until auto_stop_silence at 14:43:59.495Z (562 s), the second until a stop at 13:41:30.217Z with no lifecycle ended. The third (2026-10-01) came 5 ms after the end had already confirmed.
- Field: demonstrated. lifecycle ending_provisional led by process_audio_is_running_input_false, coordinator lifecycle_provisional_end_rescued, signal ax_leave_button_arm_failed, then no ax_leave_button_state and no lifecycle ended before the stop. Found in 2 of 3 arm failures: 2026-10-06T14:34:37.313Z and 2026-10-08T13:40:24.882Z. Snippet is from commit 8d4a9449 (identical at ae035d58), lines 149 to 155; it is not the text at HEAD.
- Verifier: Compared the throwing _start at 8d4a9449 (same as c56f2869) with HEAD's non-throwing subscribeDestroyed; reproduced all three arm failures and their surroundings from the pack. The only correction is that element is not cleared; the dead poll is the real mechanism. No arm failures occur in era B or C.
- Direction: Already fixed in E3 and HEAD. Keep it as a regression trace in the lifecycle corpus (a rescue re-arm whose subscribe throws at the moment of a real end), and rebuild the home Mac.

**D107. NEW-2: on Zoom the live mute control is readable for about a tenth of the call**

- Severity medium (confirmed). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipe/MeetingAXWindowWatcher.swift:361`. Related: D059, F003, G007, MIC7. Effort M.
- Owner sees: Mic usage or mute state is identified wrongly
- Mechanism: On every Zoom recording in the work-laptop pack (one per build era), the 1 Hz AX mute poll had no confident reading for most of the call, so the blind clear (3 consecutive blind polls, MeetingAXWindowWatcher.swift around line 360, same code and threshold at c56f2869) dropped a latched muted state 40 times out of 47 blind clears. In 38 of those 40, the next confident poll read muted again, so the dropped state was still correct. For about 88 to 91% of each Zoom call the gate had no app mute state and ran on its other signals. Why the control is unreadable (for example the auto-hiding control bar) is not established.
- Field: demonstrated. For us.zoom.xos: share of recording time between a mute_state_cleared_blind and the next mute_state_polled. FOUND: 91% (20260930-180324), 88% (20261013-170136), 89% (20261112-170131). Not established: why the control is unreadable; an AX dump of a Zoom call with the bar hidden would settle it.
- Verifier: Code: consume() increments consecutiveBlindPolls when fuse() returns nil. At blindClearThreshold (default 3, also 3 at c56f2869) it emits mute_state_cleared_blind, sets lastEmitted to nil and calls onMuteCleared (MicGate.clearAxMute). The next confident read is then always a transition, so mute_state_polled is logged. That makes 'time from a blind clear to the next mute_state_polled' a valid measure of how long the state stays un-latched. Field (my script the session scratchpad recordings segmented from recording_started to recording_stopped, events with bundle_id us.zoom.xos): 2026-09-30 (era A) 2056 s long, 13 blind clears (11 from muted), 1880 s un-latched, 91.4%. 2026-10-13 (era B) 2139 s, 28 clears (23 from muted), 1882 s, 88.0%. 2026-11-12 (era C) 1105 s, 6 clears (6 from muted), 985 s, 89.2%. Total 47 clears, 40 from muted (the field checker said 36), 4747 s un-latched (the checker said 4131). The shares match the claim. b.py: after each blind clear from muted, the next mute_state_polled read fused=muted in 38 cases, unmuted in 1, and none in 1. So the clear threw away a muted state that was still correct, and the gate went blind while the owner was in fact muted. The era C micgate_attached row has mute_button true, so the handles do resolve. The primary probe's ax_mute_button_state rows (8 across all Zoom data) are too few to refill the gap. Nothing in code re-latches during a blind stretch. I did not re-derive the silent_by_rms 74% figure. The root cause (control bar removed from the AX tree when hidden) remains an inference.
- Direction: Probe-first: dump Zoom's AX tree with the control bar hidden and shown. If the bar is the only carrier, read a control that stays in the tree (the menu bar's mute item) or hold the last confident state through blind stretches on Zoom instead of clearing it after three polls.

**D108. NEW-5: a recording resumed after an end runs with a lifecycle that already ended, so the mic release and the vanished Leave button are ignored (audit F030, F045, F046 seen together in the newest build)**

- Severity medium (confirmed). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipe/MeetingSessionController.swift:1657`. Related: F030, F045, F046, END17, END14. Effort M.
- Owner sees: Meeting end is missed (cross-symptom: the microphone evidence was right and unused)
- Mechanism: Confirmed at HEAD, but the snippet's isEngaged guard is not the root cause, it only fails to rescue the situation. Here is the chain. (1) The END17 resume path (handleMeetingStarted, recording_resumed_after_end) calls beginRecording straight from .idle. (2) While the engine is in .starting, the first shareable_content_window_present=false reading reaches PromotionEngine.handleStarting. No process-audio reading is in `states` yet (it lands 2 ms later), so veto() returns nil, and a reliable signal ends .starting directly with confirmed_by []. (3) handleMeetingEnded gets that .ended while the state machine is still .idle (state_change idle to recording comes 63 ms later). Its REC7 latch (recordingStartInFlight to endObservedDuringStart) sits only inside the .prompting/.suppressed case, so the .idle default branch drops the end with no log row. (4) The engine then stays in phase .ended: ingest returns nil for every later event, and confirmRecording is a no-op outside .starting, so no in_meeting row appears. (5) The coordinator still holds activeAdapter, so isEngaged is true and engageLifecycleIfNeeded logs already_engaged and skips. A re-engage would have reset the engine. From that point only the idle backstop or a manual stop can end the recording. This is head_only: c56f2869 has neither the resume path nor the already_engaged guard, and the work-laptop sequence was seen only in build era 3 (from 2026-11-12).
- Field: demonstrated. coordinator/recording_resumed_after_end, then lifecycle/ended with confirmed_by empty within ms, then lifecycle_engage_skipped already_engaged, then no lifecycle row until the stop while signal rows keep arriving. FOUND once in era 3: 20261112-174606 (resume 15:46:06.606Z, ended 15:46:06.614Z, mic false 15:50:34.737Z, stop 15:56:54Z). 1 of 6 era-3 recordings.
- Verifier: Code at HEAD 4949c9c0. The snippet exists at MeetingSessionController.swift:1655-1663. MeetingLifecycleCoordinator.isEngaged is `activeAdapter != nil` (line 278), and nothing disengages on .ended. PromotionEngine.handleStarting (around lines 310-340) ends .starting directly on a non-corroboration signal when veto() finds no live process-audio reading in `states`. ingest() returns nil in .ended, and confirmRecording guards on .starting. In handleMeetingEnded (line 1206 on), only .recording stops the recording. .prompting/.suppressed latch the end when recordingStartInFlight is set. The default branch (.idle) breaks silently, and the resume path's beginRecording starts from .idle. Field pack: I ran python3 -I scripts in the scratchpad d108 folder over events*.jsonl, sorted by ts. The signature is exact. At 15:46:06.598Z lifecycle/starting (teams2, pid 725). At .606Z recording_resumed_after_end (resumed_from 20261112-172307, since_end_sec 38.6). At .614Z shareable_content_window_present value false reason initial, then lifecycle/ended with leading_signal shareable_content_window_gone and confirmed_by []. At .616Z process_audio_is_running_input value true reason initial. At .677Z state_change idle to recording. At .743Z recording_started for 20261112-174606. At .813Z lifecycle_engage_skipped already_engaged. At 15:50:34.737Z process_audio false (poll) and at .823Z ax_leave_button_state invalid, with no lifecycle row after either. The next lifecycle row is idle at 15:56:54.658Z, after state_change recording to stopping at 15:56:54.489Z. That stop came about 6m20s after the mic release. Prevalence check. Definition: a lifecycle/ended followed within 2 s by state_change to=recording with no state_change in between. Across the whole pack this happened exactly 1 time, and recording_resumed_after_end also occurs exactly once. already_engaged appears 62 times in total, mostly benign, so that row alone is not the signature. Severity is lowered to medium because the race is narrow: the resume path, plus a window-gone reading ordered ahead of process audio, about 1 in 74 recordings in the pack. The impact is real, though: a dead lifecycle for the whole recording and an end that is missed despite a correct mic signal. c56f2869 check: the resume path does not exist there (it arrives with commit 01e904a5, END17), and grep finds no already_engaged guard, so the claimed path cannot run on the home Mac build.
- Direction: Do not accept an end from a signal's first reading, and make the explicit-start engage check the engine's verdict, not only whether an adapter is attached: an engaged coordinator whose verdict is ended must be re-engaged before a recording depends on it.

**D109. Teams' teardown line confirmed none of the newest build's ends, so every Teams end waited the full 60 s**

- Severity medium (partially confirmed). Location `daemon/Sources/MeetingPipeCore/Lifecycle/CallTeardownOracle.swift:139`. Related: F031, F160, F142, G053, D069, END9, END12, END16, END17, ADR 0017. Effort M.
- Owner sees: Ends are late and not smooth
- Mechanism: In build era C (from 2026-11-12 09:19Z), all 4 native Teams holds that concluded in an end got no teardown confirm and ended exactly at the 60 s dwell, even though the AX leave button and the window-gone signal agreed. The code at CallTeardownOracle.swift:139 is not itself wrong: it confirms only when the watcher's lastTeardownAt is set, and that never happened inside the dwell. The reader can work: the oracle confirmed with held_sec 0 on 2026-11-09 and 2026-11-12 07:39Z, both build B. Whether the teardown line was not yet flushed by SlimCore or was not read in build C is not established. The held tail is excluded from the transcript and summary (suggested trim, END17). So the owner-visible cost is a stop and a pipeline start about 60 s late, not post-call audio in the summary.
- Field: demonstrated. coordinator/lifecycle_end_held with oracle_available true, then lifecycle/ended exactly confirm_dwell_sec later with no lifecycle_provisional_end_confirmed and no call_teardown_observed in confirmed_by. FOUND 4 of 4 Teams ends in build C (60.01, 60.03, 60.04, 60.08 s after the mic release; log_written_sec_ago 129, 1390, 286, 603). lifecycle_provisional_end_confirmed appears twice in the whole pack, both in build B.
- Verifier: Snippet confirmed at CallTeardownOracle.swift:139. The file does not exist at c56f2869, so head_only is right. TeardownHoldPolicy.consult passes the watcher's lastTeardownAt (nil when unavailable). CallTeardownWatcher sets lastTeardownAt only from a parsed teardown line in the tailed chunk. Field (my script the session scratchpad events after the 2026-11-12T09:19:44Z app_rebuild). Teams lifecycle_end_held (oracle_available true, confirm_dwell_sec 60) followed by lifecycle/ended with no lifecycle_provisional_end_confirmed: 14:32:36 to 14:33:36 (60.07 s, log_written_sec_ago 129), 14:53:37 to 14:54:37 (60.03 s, 1390), 15:44:27 to 15:45:27 (60.04 s, 286), 2026-11-13 07:42:25 to 07:43:25 (60.01 s, 603). Each ended row's confirmed_by lists ax_leave_button_invalid and shareable_content_window_gone. The other Teams holds in era C released (call came back) and are not ends. lifecycle_provisional_end_confirmed appears twice in the whole pack, both oracle=teardown_observed with held_sec 0, at 2026-11-09T16:02Z and 2026-11-12T07:39Z, both before the era C rebuild. There is no call_teardown_observed action in the pack at all, so the 'not in confirmed_by' part of the signature carries no information. 16,861 call_teardown_source_missing rows confirmed. held_end_trimmed mode 'suggested' follows every era C end, and the controller (MeetingSessionController.swift around lines 788 to 800) keeps the held tail on disk but excludes it from the transcript and summary. The latency is real and demonstrated. The defect sits in the premise that the line is on disk at the AX edge (or in the watcher), not in decide().
- Direction: Stop treating the teardown line as the normal route, or measure why it does not arrive (flush or read). Make the pair's second class the Leave control rather than the window stamp (F031), then size the dwell from data: the only two mid-call mic releases in the pack lasted 23.1 s and at most 23.54 s (the END15 case), so 60 s is conservative and anything under 30 s is not safe. Keep the trim so a wrong call costs only the tail.

**D004. Detection needs the Meet tab selected, in a window that is on screen, at two separate reads**

- Severity low (partially confirmed). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipeCore/Lifecycle/Signals/ShareableContentSignal.swift:281`. Related: START2, END5, F032, F002. Effort L.
- Owner sees: No prompt when the call is in a background tab, a minimised window, or on another Space or full-screen Space. The prompt appears only after returning to the Meet tab and waiting for the next poll.
- Mechanism: A plain-browser call only reaches lifecycle starting once ScreenCaptureKit lists an on-screen window from that browser whose title (the selected tab) matches a meeting pattern (ShareableContentSignal.swift:149-151 and 158-160, the same onScreenWindowsOnly: true call at c56f2869 line 226). Discovery uses AX window titles (MeetingSourceScanner.collectAXWindowTitles). The claim that both reads must hold at the same moment is overstated. After discovery engages the browser adapter, the SCK read polls at 1 Hz and keeps going while the engine is idle, so a window that comes on screen later raises starting from the poll alone. The field pack shows this: starting at 2026-11-11T14:12:18Z came from a poll 12 min 47 s after the engage at 13:59:31Z. A background tab is already blocked by the AX title gate, so the on-screen gate adds nothing there. The SCK gate is the only blocker for a window that AX lists but SCK does not, most plausibly a minimised one. That case follows from the code and has no field evidence. When the user returns, discovery (3 s poll) or the running 1 Hz poll produces the prompt within seconds. The same on-screen-only read has a separate end-side effect, seen in era 2: at 2026-11-11T14:14:28.159Z the SCK candidate list for com.google.Chrome was empty, and the poll ended the engine and stopped a manual recording. This is END territory, not the start symptom, and END17 (era 3 and HEAD) holds a recording-time window-gone as a weak end.
- Field: demonstrated (3 intervals where a live call's window left the on-screen list; 1 with a consequence; 0 start misses attributable). 2026-11-11T14:14:28.159Z the candidate list for Chrome was empty (no Chrome window on screen), matching again 8.2 s later; that single read ended the engine and cut the recording. Tab-away intervals of 2.9 s inside named spans at 2026-10-06T09:00:37Z and 2026-10-07T09:16:12Z show the read follows the selected tab. The tracer's proposed signature (browser pick, then no_match reason initial, no starting) fires 4 times, not 0, but in each a Meet window is on screen with a code-less title, so those belong to D001. No background-tab or other-Space start miss can be shown, because none leaves a row.
- Build eras: Era 1: 2 tab-away intervals. Era 2: 1 empty list. Era 3: none observed. On-screen-only fetch unchanged at HEAD (ShareableContentSignal.swift:281).
- Verifier: Code (HEAD 4949c9c0). ShareableContentSignal.defaultFetch calls getExcludingDesktopWindows(false, onScreenWindowsOnly: true). evaluate() counts a window as present only when bundleIdentifier == context.bundleID and titleMatch(title). BrowserMeetingLifecycleAdapter maps present to .browserTabTitle live or ended. That is the only primary signal for a plain browser, because WindowTitleSignal is wired only when handle.meetingWindow exists (PWAs). The scanner admits a plain browser through decideBrowserAdmission over AX kAXWindowsAttribute titles, and in every era it uses the same matcher set as the adapter: I checked commits 2e3070a1, 8d4a9449 and b7f52334, and the googleMeet brand+code rule is the same in all three. c56f2869 has the same onScreenWindowsOnly call (line 226) and the same predicate (line 117), so applies_to is both. Refutation points: (1) once engaged, the adapter's 1 Hz poll keeps running while the engine is idle, so the two reads do not need to coincide. (2) For a background tab, the AX selected-tab title already fails discovery. The defect claimed here is therefore limited to windows AX lists and SCK does not, such as minimised ones. For other Spaces, AX listing is platform behaviour I could not read from code. (3) Browsers expose no per-tab state, which BrowserMeetingLifecycleAdapter documents; this is a platform limit more than a code bug. Field pack, work laptop. Script d004/q3.py ran over all events*.jsonl. Definition: a shareable_content_no_match with reason initial for a browser bundle. Each one was checked privately against the googleMeet, browserTeams, webex and huddle matchers and the toml URL fragments, and only counts were printed. Result: 4 rows (2026-10-06T08:58:38Z, 2026-10-07T09:03:34Z, 2026-10-21T12:32:54Z, 2026-11-11T13:59:31Z), all com.google.Chrome. Each followed a discovery_shadow_pick with winner_kind browser within 3 s. In each, SCK listed 2 or 3 on-screen Chrome windows: 0 matched any adapter matcher, 0 contained a URL fragment, and exactly 1 contained "meet" without a code. A Meet window was therefore on screen, and the miss was a title difference, AX coded and SCK code-less within about 75 ms, not a visibility gap. The 11-11 case is a real start miss: a manual recording_started at 13:59:52.561Z, sidecar 20261111-155951 with no source, came 21 s after the pick, while SCK kept reading a code-less Meet window on screen until it matched at 14:12:18Z. That points to D001 (Meet title without a code), not D004. Start misses attributable to the on-screen gate: 0 found, and a background-tab or minimised start miss leaves no row by construction. Chrome is a small share of this laptop's use: 7 of 417 prompts and 1 of 74 recording_started (re-counted). So the start symptom is code_derived only and low severity. The checker's 14:14:28Z empty-candidate end is confirmed (q4.py timeline: no_match with n=0, then window_present false, then lifecycle ended, then state recording to stopping, then recording_stopped manual), but it is an end-side effect in era 2.
- Direction: Take presence from something that does not depend on what is drawn: the capturing process for whether a call exists, and a per-tab URL read (scripting or AX document URL, pending the DET6 measurement) for which tab it is.

**D009. An unavailable first ScreenCaptureKit read leaves the window signal unarmed for that engage**

- Severity low (unverified low). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipeCore/Lifecycle/Signals/ShareableContentSignal.swift:204`. Related: PERF19, PERF15. Effort S.
- Owner sees: On the installed build a slow or refused first read delays the prompt until the next scan re-engages, 3 to 12 s later.
- Mechanism: Read at c56f2869: evaluate returned on a nil probe before adjustCadence, the only place a poll timer was armed, and start ran the probe synchronously on main. Read at HEAD: noteUnavailable arms the 1 s poll and the first evaluate runs off main (PERF19, PERF15). I checked that the HEAD path does arm (lines 204-218).
- Field: demonstrated (1). 2026-09-30T13:17:46.848Z shareable_content_unavailable reason initial for Chrome, then 0 Chrome signal rows for 33.3 s until the app was relaunched (permission_granted burst at 13:18:20Z), while the same tab matched on the first read of the engages before and after. The window is short, so this is one consistent instance, not a long outage. Source at ae035d58 and 8d4a9449 returns on a nil probe before adjustCadence; f2d65038 and HEAD arm the poll in noteUnavailable.
- Build eras: Era 1: 1. Era 2: 0 instances, defect still in the code. Era 3: fixed (PERF19, ba65a7d8).
- Direction: Install a current build.

**D017. The 16 second recording: Zoom's window left the on-screen list and an empty Leave re-walk 10 ms later was taken as proof the call had ended**

- Severity low (partially confirmed). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipe/MeetingSessionController.swift:1238`. Related: END8, END9, END14, END17, G001, F002, F030, F031, F033. Effort S.
- Owner sees: Recording stops seconds after it starts while the call goes on; no prompt for a minute; then prompts.
- Mechanism: The mechanism is real, but only in builds before END9 (86269d3a, 2026-08-20), which means the home Mac's c56f2869 build. In those builds rescueProvisionalEnd treated an empty Leave re-walk as proof of an end and called confirmProvisionalEnd. The 16 s chop is in the HOME Mac's ~/Library/Logs/MeetingPipe/events.jsonl (2026-10-13 08:03:05 to 08:03:21Z, us.zoom.xos), not in the work-laptop field pack. Every work-laptop build era postdates END9, so this path cannot fire there. The pack has 0 ax_leave_rewalk rows and no Zoom recording at that time. At HEAD an empty walk just returns. A window-gone end that a live process-audio reading contradicts is vetoed (END14). Any other weak end is held with the recorder running (END17). The window probe is still on-screen-only at HEAD (ShareableContentSignal.swift:282), but that residual is a separate issue (F002), not this defect.
- Field: demonstrated (0). recording_started with bundle_id manual, then lifecycle starting for another bundle with no in_meeting, then lifecycle ended with empty confirmed_by followed within milliseconds by state_change recording to stopping. Found once in 4 manual recordings: stem 20261111-155951, Chrome starting 14:12:18.901Z, ended 14:14:28.163Z (leading_signal browser_tab_title_left_meet_pattern), stopping 14:14:28.169Z. 0 meeting_end_ignored_other_app and 0 lifecycle_late_start_confirmed rows in the pack. Transcript class: last segment ends at 876.9 s of 876.9 s, no farewell-class word anywhere.
- Build eras: none
- Verifier: Code: in c56f2869, MeetingSessionController.rescueProvisionalEnd logs lifecycle_provisional_end_confirmed and calls confirmProvisionalEnd() when findAllLeaveButtons returns empty (inside the guard at about lines 1234-1242). git log -S shows END9 86269d3a (2026-08-20) replaced that path with beginTeardownHold. At HEAD 4949c9c0 (lines 1810-1817) the empty-walk branch only returns. PromotionEngine.swift:235-238 and :411-415 at HEAD veto a lone window-gone end while process audio reads live. Other weak ends open a hold and are confirmed only by the teardown oracle or the engine dwells. Log, home Mac (~/Library/Logs/MeetingPipe/events.jsonl, read by me): recording_started us.zoom.xos 08:03:05.375Z. process_audio_is_running_input stayed true from 08:03:01.829Z with no change logged. shareable_content_no_match and window_present=false at 08:03:21.966/.967Z. ending_provisional (leading shareable_content_window_gone) at .968, lifecycle_provisional_end_confirmed with no oracle attribute at .978, then ended confirmed_by [ax_leave_rewalk] and recording to stopping at .979 (wallclock 16.67 s). Next came candidate_dropped at 08:03:28.8Z and discovery_shadow_pick at 08:03:32.1Z with no prompt. The next prompt_shown was at 08:05:35.9Z. That matches the claimed signature exactly. Work-laptop pack (my grep over fieldpack/logs/events*.jsonl): 0 rows containing ax_leave_rewalk. The Zoom recording_started rows are at 2026-09-30T15:03Z, 2026-10-13T14:01Z and 2026-11-12T15:01Z, with none at 08:03Z. There are only 2 lifecycle_provisional_end_confirmed rows in total. The field checker's "demonstrated" item, a manual-plus-Chrome tab-title end on stem 20261111-155951, is a different mechanism and does not involve this code path. Conclusion: the defect is real and its signature is demonstrated on the home Mac's stale build. It is fixed at HEAD and in all three work-laptop build eras, so it does not cause the owner's daily work-laptop symptom. Severity is lowered to low for that reason.
- Direction: Install HEAD. Then ask ScreenCaptureKit for all windows rather than on-screen ones, or stop using window presence as an end signal for apps that have a process-audio read.

**D020. Nothing is captured before consent: the first sample trails the call by the scan wait plus the owner's reaction, and there is no pre-roll**

- Severity low (partially confirmed). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipe/MeetingSessionController.swift:570`. Related: PERF6, PERF15, F004, F045. Effort M.
- Owner sees: Recordings begin mid-greeting; the first seconds or minutes of every call are missing.
- Mechanism: Capture starts only in beginRecording's audioRecorder.start, after consent (MeetingSessionController.swift:570). daemon/Sources has no pre-roll or ring buffer: the only grep hit is an unrelated comment in InputDeviceRecovery.swift. Work laptop pack: I paired each prompt_shown with the next recording_started for the same bundle (unanswered prompts reset the pair, gaps of 120 s or more excluded). That gives n=61, median 4.4 s, p90 12.7 s, max 24.0 s, min 1.5 s. Typically a few seconds of the opening are lost, sometimes tens of seconds. 'Minutes of every call' is overstated, except when a prompt is answered very late or never. This is the deliberate consent-before-capture design, not a regression.
- Field: demonstrated (60 answered native prompts, 52 Teams calls recorded on the prompt). Prompt to recording_started: median 4.0 s, p90 13.0 s, max 24.0 s; first sighting to recording_started: median 5.5 s, p90 17.0 s, max 26.1 s. In era C two of five answers took 15.0 s and 24.0 s (stems 20261112-163040, 20261112-170131). 18 of 58 prompt-answered transcripts open with speech inside 3 s. No pre-roll event or buffer exists in any era.
- Build eras: A, B, C
- Verifier: Snippet and mechanism confirmed. The latency appears in every era of the work-laptop event log, so the symptom (first seconds missing) is shown in the log, but its size is seconds, not minutes. I did not measure scan-wait latency because the log has no join timestamp. Pre-roll before consent would conflict with the consent model, so this is a product tradeoff and I rate it low.
- Direction: Open a call session on the process-audio edge and capture into a short in-memory ring (or a provisional file) at once; consent decides whether it is kept. Keep it off under the regulated and NDA postures, where ADR 0016 forbids audio at rest before consent.

**D026. Skip, cooldown and resume live in memory keyed by bundle id, so a relaunch forgets them and a different call in the same app inherits them**

- Severity low (unverified low). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipe/RepromptCooldown.swift:5`. Related: DET13, DET9, DET14. Effort M.
- Owner sees: After restarting the app the call you skipped prompts again; a second call joined within a minute of the first gets no prompt.
- Mechanism: Read: RepromptCooldown and SkippedMeetingLatch are dictionaries from bundle id to Date, with no persistence and no meeting identity; the latch's own comment names the blind spot (a different meeting in the same app within the grace inherits it). DET13 (not installed) keeps the suppressed sighting attachable from the menu but still shows no prompt. daemon.log on 2026-09-11 has a prompt 49 s after a timeout for the same bundle, inside both windows; a relaunch would explain it and nothing else I read does, but that day's event log no longer exists.
- Field: demonstrated (6 Teams calls inherited a suppression (2 latch, 4 cooldown); relaunch half 0). Inherit: picks at 2026-10-20T14:53:25Z and 2026-10-21T14:56:31Z, 23 s and 8 s after the previous unanswered call dropped to title only, raised no lifecycle and no prompt for 81 s and 1,538 s because the skip latch was inside its 60 s grace and each confident scan refreshed it. Cooldown: picks 6 to 54 s after the stop decision on 2026-10-01, 10-22, 11-05 and 11-06 got their prompt 40, 71, 56 and 10 s late and all 4 timed out. The relaunch half is not shown: no prompt came within 60 s of a native timeout or skip across 12 launches, and the one Chrome re-prompt with a launch row before it (2026-09-30T13:18:37Z) had already re-engaged 22 s before that launch.
- Build eras: A, B; latch code unchanged in C and HEAD
- Direction: Key decisions on a call session (pid plus mic-hold span) and persist the open session across a relaunch.

**D048. Installed build: one empty AX walk confirms a native end within 10 ms while the app still holds the mic**

- Severity low (partially confirmed). Location `daemon/Sources/MeetingPipe/MeetingSessionController.swift:1238`. Related: END9, END10, END14, END17, F002, G006. Effort S.
- Owner sees: A Zoom or Teams recording stops seconds or minutes into a live call. The owner is then prompted again and again for the same call.
- Mechanism: The defect is real, but only in the stale c56f2869 build on the home Mac. At c56f2869, MeetingSessionController.swift:1238 treats an empty Leave-button re-walk in rescueProvisionalEnd as corroboration, logs lifecycle_provisional_end_confirmed and calls confirmProvisionalEnd(). END9 (86269d3a, 2026-08-20) removed this path. Every work-laptop build era starts later than that: the oldest is END10-era, from 78219db3 on. 78219db3 and 8d4a9449 (END14) open beginTeardownHold on an empty walk, and HEAD (01e904a5 onward, line 1810) just returns. The work-laptop pack has 0 rows containing ax_leave_rewalk among 114,553 events, and none of its 381 lifecycle ended rows carries that confirmation. The 2026-10-13 08:03Z Zoom signature is in the home Mac's ~/Library/Logs/MeetingPipe/events.jsonl, not in the work-laptop pack. So this defect cannot cause the early ends the owner sees on the work laptop.
- Field: demonstrated (0 of 381 lifecycle ended rows). coordinator recording_started with bundle_id manual; later lifecycle starting for some bundle with no prompt_shown; then lifecycle ended with confirmed_by [] for that bundle, followed within 10 ms by state_change recording to stopping. Found once: lifecycle ended at 2026-11-11T14:14:28.163Z, leading browser_tab_title_left_meet_pattern. The other three source-less recordings saw no such end.
- Build eras: none (fixed in all work-laptop builds)
- Verifier: Code. The snippet exists at c56f2869:daemon/Sources/MeetingPipe/MeetingSessionController.swift lines 1233 to 1242. When the walk finds no Leave button, that version logs lifecycle_provisional_end_confirmed and calls confirmProvisionalEnd(). Checked with `git merge-base --is-ancestor`: c56f2869 comes before END9 commit 86269d3a. At 86269d3a, 78219db3 (END10) and 8d4a9449 (END14), the empty branch calls beginTeardownHold(context:) instead. At HEAD (line 1810), it just returns with the END9 comment. The only remaining caller of lifecycle_provisional_end_confirmed at HEAD is consultTeardownOracle (line 1897), and it fires only on a TeardownHoldPolicy confirm verdict. So the mechanism is gone from every work-laptop era. Field pack. I sorted all four events files by ts, 114,553 events. 0 rows contain ax_leave_rewalk. lifecycle ended confirmed_by breaks down as: [] 321, [ax_leave_button_invalid] 39, [shareable_content_window_gone] 14, both of those together 4, [process_audio_is_running_input_false] 3. lifecycle_provisional_end_confirmed appears 2 times. Both are com.microsoft.teams2, oracle teardown_observed, on 2026-11-09 and 2026-11-12. The pack has no events at 2026-10-13T08:0x; its Zoom recording_started that day is at 14:01:36Z. Home Mac log. I found the claimed sequence there: recording_started 08:03:05.375Z, shareable_content_window_present false at 21.967Z, ending_provisional at .968, lifecycle_provisional_end_confirmed at .978, lifecycle ended confirmed_by [ax_leave_rewalk] at .979, then candidate_dropped no_confident_winner from 08:03:28Z. The latest process_audio_is_running_input row before the end was true. So applies_to fixed_at_head_not_installed is correct for the home Mac. The field checker's "demonstrated" line is unrelated: a manual-source end on 2026-11-11 with confirmed_by []. It is not this mechanism. For the work-laptop symptom this defect is not causal. Severity drops to low because the home Mac is thin-use and the code is fixed at HEAD.
- Direction: Install a current build. Keep this trace in the detection corpus as a raw replay: zero on-screen windows, live mic, must not end.

**D049. After a recorded call ends the menu still offers to record it, because the stop re-adds the candidate DET14 just cleared**

- Severity low (unverified low). Location `daemon/Sources/MeetingPipe/MeetingSessionController.swift:741`. Related: DET14, DET13, END13 item 5, DET7. Effort S.
- Owner sees: For about 24 s after every recorded call ends (longer if discovery re-sights the app during the 60 s cooldown) the menu shows 'Record this <app> meeting'. During a hold the menu-bar title still reads Recording; only the HUD says Confirming end, with no countdown.
- Mechanism: Read: `handleMeetingEnded` calls `noteMeetingEnded`, DET14's clear, and then `stopRecording`, whose first act for a sourced recording is `noteSuppressedCandidate(source, origin: .cooldown)` (END13 item 5, added two weeks after DET14). On the recording path the clear is therefore undone in the same run-loop turn, and the candidate lives `cooldownCandidateGraceSec` (two idle polls, 24 s) from each write. The code comment calls the order deliberate; the effect is that DET14's promise holds only for ends that arrive while not recording.
- Field: demonstrated (40 of 41 lifecycle-ended sourced stops in B and C; 47 rows in all). coordinator/stopped_candidate_retained (grace_sec 24) lands within 1 s after lifecycle/ended on 40 of 41 such stops (the 41st has a 5.9 s gap between end and stop). The harm claimed (menu still offering a finished call) leaves no row. The benefit is in the log once: record_attached 22.9 s after the false end of 20261022-150025.
- Build eras: B and C (action first seen 2026-10-12T14:47Z)
- Direction: Skip the stop-time retention when the stop was caused by a confirmed lifecycle end; keep it for user stops and backstop stops, where a false end is the thing being bridged.

**D052. The first mic verdict of a recording is dropped as a duplicate of the previous recording's last verdict**

- Severity low (partially confirmed). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipeCore/MicGate/MicGate.swift:310`. Related: F004, END7, TECH-END3. Effort S.
- Owner sees: The idle 'Still in a meeting?' nudge and the 15 minute auto-stop do not start counting until the mic first reads loud. If a meeting end is missed and the room stays quiet afterwards (the owner walks away, which is the case the backstop exists for), the recording runs until someone stops it by hand. Under a regulated or NDA workflow the mic is written as silence for the same stretch either way.
- Mechanism: The dedupe across recordings is real at HEAD and at c56f2869. MicGate.lastVerdict is set only in publish() (MicGate.swift:38, 310-311). stop() resets state and the probes but never lastVerdict. start() calls stop() and then update { rmsState = .closed }. With axMute nil that decides .silentByRMS(dwellMillis: 0), because rmsCloseDwellMillis is never written. If the previous recording ended on silent_by_rms dwell 0, publish() drops that first verdict. The session sets latestVerdict = nil (MeetingSessionController resetMicGateBackstop). The 1 Hz onMicLevel feed then returns early, so the idle backstop gets nothing until a different verdict arrives. On the work laptop the duplicate is common: 16 recordings followed a last verdict of silent_by_rms dwell 0, and in 14 of them the first emitted verdict was something else. In every one of those 14, a different verdict (muted_by_app, hot confidently_unmuted or hot rms_above_open_threshold) arrived 0.14 to 3.27 s after recording_started, so the backstop lost only seconds. The forgotten quiet recording is not shown in the pack. It needs a source without AX (browser, or a session where the gate engages without an AX read), a previous recording that ended silent dwell 0, and a mic that never crosses the RMS open threshold or HAL VAD for the whole recording. The long verdict-less starts (manual, 48.4, 11.5, 0.1 and 22.1 s) come from MicGate never starting for a manual source (F004), not from this dedupe. The Safari recordings cited as evidence are not in the work-laptop pack: none of its 74 recording_started rows is from Safari.
- Field: demonstrated (14 of 16 recordings that followed a last verdict of silent_by_rms dwell 0 did not emit it first). The cross-recording dedupe is real: the 2 exceptions (20261012-163138, 20261021-120034) each followed a daemon relaunch. It did no harm here: on Teams and Zoom the first different verdict (muted_by_app 10, hot rms 3, confidently_unmuted 1) came 0.1 to 3.3 s after the start. The long verdict-less starts are the four manual recordings (48.4, 11.5, 0.1, 22.1 s), where MicGate never starts at all (audit F004). The Chrome recording emitted silent_by_rms at 0.0 s.
- Build eras: eras 1, 2 and 3 (4, 8 and 2 of the 14)
- Verifier: Code. I read HEAD MicGate.swift. lastVerdict starts as .uncertain(not_started) at line 38 and is assigned only inside publish(). stop() (lines 138-148) clears state and lastLogReadingAt but not lastVerdict. start() runs stop(), probe starts, rmsGate.reset() and update { $0.rmsState = .closed }. decide() returns .silentByRMS(dwellMillis: state.rmsCloseDwellMillis) when rms is closed, VAD is not true and axMute is nil. grep shows rmsCloseDwellMillis only defaults to 0 and is never assigned elsewhere, so dwell is always 0. In MeetingSessionController.resetMicGateBackstop, latestVerdict = nil. The onMicLevel closure guards on latestVerdict and returns when it is nil. latestVerdict is set only from the micGate.verdicts stream, which publish() feeds after the dedupe. c56f2869 has the same lines (MicGate.swift 38/119/138/145/310-311, and in its controller latestVerdict at lines 104/136/999/1062). Field. I ran python3 -I over events*.jsonl, sorted by ts. For each recording_started to recording_stopped pair, I took the last micgate.verdict_changed before the start and the first one inside the recording. Result: 16 recordings followed silent_by_rms dwell 0, and 14 of them first emitted a different verdict. That proves the duplicate was suppressed. The 2 exceptions re-emitted silent_by_rms; one coincides with an app_rebuild relaunch on 2026-10-12. For the 14, the delay to the first emitted verdict ran from 0.142 to 3.267 s, on Teams and on one Zoom recording (2026-11-12T15:01:31Z, 0.104 s). The AX mute probe or the RMS gate breaks the tie almost at once, so the backstop starvation lasted seconds. The only Chrome recording (2026-10-05) followed a hot verdict and was not exposed. The manual recordings with long verdict-less starts follow a hot previous verdict, so they are not this mechanism. They are F004 territory: MicGate is not engaged for them. Severity. The backstop gap opens only when the whole recording stays below the RMS open threshold and has no AX or VAD reading. That is a narrow case on top of an already missed end. Under the regulated gate the recorder's .uncertain(not_started) latch zero-fills, as silent_by_rms would, so audio output is the same either way. Mechanism confirmed, owner symptom not observed in any build era, so severity drops from high to low.
- Direction: Give the verdict stream a session: reset lastVerdict in start(), always yield the first verdict, and have the backstop treat 'no verdict yet' as silent instead of skipping the tick. Pin it with a test that runs two sessions through one MicGate.

**D055. HAL voice activity is never available, so the 'VAD-gated' idle backstop and both VAD heuristics are a raw level gate**

- Severity low (confirmed). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipeCore/MicGate/Probes/HALVoiceActivityProbe.swift:71`. Related: TECH-END3, MIC10, F035, F037, DET10 (2026-10-25 amendment). Effort M.
- Owner sees: A forgotten recording in a room with any sound above the threshold (typing, a door, another conversation) never reaches the 15 minute auto-stop; a quiet-voiced owner is treated as silent. Nothing in the UI says the voice detector is absent.
- Mechanism: HALVoiceActivityProbe only reads kAudioDevicePropertyVoiceActivityDetectionEnable and never sets it; when it reads off, it emits vad_unsupported and returns, so on the work laptop the MicGate verdict never has a VAD input and hot means the RMS level gate (or the unmuted floor). That VAD is absent is demonstrated in the log in all three eras. That this makes the 15 minute idle backstop miss a forgotten recording, or mute a quiet speaker, is not shown in the pack.
- Field: demonstrated (vad_unsupported at 70 of 70 MicGate starts; 0 vad_state; 0 vad contradiction clears). Holds on the built-in mic and on the bluetooth input (2026-11-04 to 11-06), for Teams, Zoom and Chrome. So every hot verdict in the pack is the level gate or the confidently-unmuted floor (hot rms 47% and confidently_unmuted 15% of Teams time), and F035 and F037 cannot be what the owner sees.
- Build eras: all three (23, 41 and 6 starts)
- Verifier: Snippet at HALVoiceActivityProbe.swift:71 confirmed; defaultEnableProbe reads the enable property only. Present at c56f2869 too (vad_unsupported found), so both. Field, my script: vad_unsupported E1 23, E2 41, E3 6 (70 total), vad_state 0, mute_state_cleared_vad_contradiction 0. Absence of VAD is demonstrated; the owner-visible consequence (backstop never firing because noise keeps the level gate hot) has no field case I found, so the symptom is code-derived. Downgraded to low because the fallback to the level gate is the documented degraded mode and no harm is shown.
- Direction: Stop depending on a device property the daemon cannot turn on. Run speech detection on the daemon's own samples, and log one line per recording saying which speech evidence was actually live.

**D057. HAL listeners survive unsubscribe: one more stale default-input listener after every recording**

- Severity low (confirmed). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipeCore/Infra/RealCoreAudioBackend.swift:32`. Related: CONC9, PERF16, F033. Effort S.
- Owner sees: None directly. On the installed build stale listeners keep changing MicGate state while idle (which is how the previous-verdict duplicate above was set up on 2026-10-15) and multiply HAL work with every engage; over a long-lived daemon this grows without bound.
- Mechanism: Default-input listeners keep firing after the bus logs them unsubscribed. In era 2 (build of 2026-10-12, before CONC9 12c88a38 landed on 10-26, so with no generation guard) every stale handler re-subscribed the mute listener on each idle default-input change, and the burst size grew with the number of recordings made in the process. HEAD's CONC9 generation check makes stale handlers return early, so the listeners still leak but cause no state change. The block-identity cause is still an inference.
- Field: demonstrated (era 2: 110 out-of-recording mute subscribe attempts in 32 bursts; era 1: 7,637 unresolved rows in 4.9 s). Era 2: between 2026-11-04T12:20:02Z and 2026-11-07T08:48:37Z the HAL mute listener re-subscribed with no recording running, 1 per change on 2026-11-04 before that day's recordings, 3 after them, 6 to 8 on 2026-11-05 and 11-06, 10 on 2026-11-07, although the matching unsubscribes were logged (mute 180 of 180, default-input 69 of 70). Era 1: at 2026-10-12T08:20:22Z the process-list change produced 22.5 unresolved rows per listener subscribed since launch (340), then the daemon relaunched. Era 3: 6 mute subscribes, all inside recordings, no burst in 2 days; HEAD's generation guards would hide it and RealCoreAudioBackend.swift is unchanged since 2026-05-28.
- Build eras: eras 1 and 2; not seen in era 3
- Verifier: I confirmed the code: RealCoreAudioBackend discards the remove call's OSStatus and has been unchanged since 2026-05-28, so the same code is at c56f2869 and at HEAD. Definitions for my script: a burst is mute subscribe_attempt rows on halbus within 0.6 s of each other, all outside any recording_started to recording_stopped window. Result: 11-04 had 13 bursts of 1, then 3 bursts of 3 after that day's afternoon recordings. 11-05 had bursts of 4 and 7. 11-06 had bursts of 7 and one of 8 after the 07:30 recording. 11-07 had 10 and 10. Each recording logged a matching dIn unsubscribed (10 subscribed, 10 unsubscribed between 11-04 and 11-07), and there was no app_rebuild in between. CoreAudio is the only thing that invokes those blocks, so a stale listener fires, which means the removal did not take. HEAD (CONC9) adds a generation guard in HALSystemMuteProbe that hides the effect. The owner sees no symptom and the leak costs HAL work, so severity is low.
- Direction: Register one permanent listener per (object, selector) inside the bus and fan out in process, so nothing is removed per subscription; check and log the remove status where removal remains; add one test against the real backend.

**D058. A browser call has no mute oracle, and the design document describes one the code does not attempt**

- Severity low (confirmed). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipe/MeetingAXHandleBuilder.swift:62`. Related: MIC7 (partial), END5, F024. Effort L.
- Owner sees: Muting in Meet changes nothing in MeetingPipe: what the owner says to someone in the room while muted is recorded, transcribed and summarized as their speech, and a 'redact muted spans' workflow has nothing to redact.
- Mechanism: For every browser bundle the handle builder returns an empty MicGateAdapterHandle, and no code scrapes the Meet or Teams-web mute button, although docs/architecture/signal-fusion-and-mic-gating.md lines 95 and 156 say an opportunistic scrape is attempted. A browser recording therefore has no app-mute oracle and is gated by RMS only. The owner-visible cost is small on the work laptop: 1 browser recording in 74, and capture-first mode keeps all audio regardless.
- Field: demonstrated (1 of 1 browser recordings). 20261005-180843 (com.google.Chrome, 13.4 min): meet_adapter_no_ax_signal, vad_unsupported, mute_state_cleared_blind with previous null, 0 mute_state_polled, 0 ax_mute_button_state.
- Build eras: era 1 (the only browser recording)
- Verifier: I confirmed the browser branch at MeetingAXHandleBuilder.swift:62 at HEAD, and the same code is at c56f2869. The doc claim at lines 95 and 156 is present and no scrape code exists. Field: in the only Chrome recording (2026-10-05T15:08:43Z to 15:22:10Z, era 1), micgate events were vad_unsupported 1, meet_adapter_no_ax_signal 1, mute_state_cleared_blind 1 (poll_count 3, previous null) and 0 mute_state_polled. The claimed Safari recordings are not in this pack and come from the other Mac. The owner symptom (muted asides transcribed) needs redaction to be the expectation, but every meta has workflow_nda_mode False and the mode is capture-first, which records muted speech for native apps too. So the gap matters only to a future redact-muted workflow.
- Direction: Decide it: either build the content-script oracle MIC7 already validated, or state plainly that browser mute is unknown, stop starting the poller for browsers, and offer the off-the-record hotkey as the only control.

**D059. Blind clear drops a confirmed app mute in every capture mode**

- Severity low (confirmed). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipe/MeetingSessionController.swift:1465`. Related: F003, G007, MIC6, MIC10, ADR 0016. Effort S.
- Owner sees: Only under an NDA or regulated workflow: speech during a muted aside is written to disk when the meeting app's controls are unreadable for 3 s. In the default mode nothing audible changes.
- Mechanism: This duplicates the independent audit's F003 and G007, which call it a known, accepted MIC6/ADR-0016 trade-off. onMuteCleared calls clearAxMute with no capturesLosslessly gate, while onStaleMuteContradiction and blindOnWindowDisagreement both have one. Blind clears of a latched muted state happen often in the field, but the harm (muted speech kept under the NDA or regulated gate) cannot occur on the work laptop, because no meeting there ran with NDA mode.
- Field: demonstrated (71 mid-call blind clears of a muted state in 19 recordings; 67 re-read as muted; 5,349 s un-latched). Teams 35 clears in 16 recordings (1,217 s), for example 20261012-163138 (7 clears, 95 s) and 20261023-173034 (198 s); Zoom 36 clears in 3 recordings (4,131 s). Right after the clear the verdict went to silent_by_rms in 50 and to hot rms in 20. Capture mode here is capture-first with no auto redaction, so nothing was removed or leaked; the regulated-gate variant the tracer describes is not observable (regulated off in 113 of 113 metas).
- Build eras: all three (19, 48, 4)
- Verifier: I confirmed the ungated wiring at MeetingSessionController.swift:1465 at HEAD and at c56f2869 line 1092. The watcher fires once per blind streak once it reaches blindClearThreshold. Script count, defined as mute_state_cleared_blind rows inside a recording_started to recording_stopped window with previous == muted: 82 (Teams 42, Zoom 40), plus 24 with previous unmuted and 2 with null. The blind clear itself is therefore demonstrated. But all 113 metas carry workflow_nda_mode False, and in capture-first mode nothing is redacted, so the privacy symptom is code-derived only.
- Direction: Move every discredit decision into one pure policy function of (capture mode, evidence) in Core and gate the blind clear like its siblings.

**D060. An AX mute reading refused while the Teams log is fresh is never delivered**

- Severity low (unverified low). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipeCore/MicGate/MicGate.swift:200`. Related: F036, G027, MIC8. Effort S.
- Owner sees: Teams only: after a quick unmute then re-mute where Teams' log misses the second line, the app stays 'unmuted' for the whole muted stretch. Visible only under redaction or the regulated gate.
- Mechanism: Read, building on F036 and G027: both AX producers record a reading as sent before calling the sink (the watcher sets lastEmitted, the probe sets lastEvent), and applyMuteReading returns nothing to them when the fusion refuses it, so MIC8's stated 5 s correction bound is not implemented. No Teams call exists in this log.
- Field: demonstrated (16 suppressed AX readings contradicted the gate; 0 corrected inside 5.5 s; 10 corrected after 7 to 993 s; 6 never). Examples: 2026-10-12T15:30:39Z (unmuted suppressed, never delivered, 1,995 s to the end), 2026-10-07T13:22:02Z (699 s), 2026-10-07T14:48:19Z (993 s), 2026-11-12T15:39:47Z (era 3, 176 s). This is the main field mechanism, not a low one, and it is driven by late log lines rather than missing ones (see NEW-1).
- Build eras: all three (6, 9 and 1 of the 16)
- Direction: Keep the last refused AX reading inside MicGate and apply it when the log window lapses.

**D070. No start or stop carries its cause, so a wrong end is never flagged and a hand stop is invisible in the log**

- Severity low (confirmed). Location `daemon/Sources/MeetingPipe/Notifier.swift:109`. Related: F006, UX93, END17, F046. Effort M.
- Owner sees: Every stop looks the same: 'Recording stopped, Processing <file>'. A 16 s fragment is processed and published like a meeting, and nothing says the app's call controls were found again 11 s later.
- Mechanism: stopRecording takes no cause, toggleManual logs nothing before it, recording_stopped carries no cause attribute, and notifyProcessing (Notifier.swift:108 to 110 at HEAD, :103 at c56f2869) posts the same 'Recording stopped' text for every route. In the field pack 11 of 74 recording->stopping transitions have no cause row (lifecycle/ended, auto_stop_silence, force_stop, low space or silence action) in the 3 s before them: 1 in build A, 8 in build B, 2 in build C. This is an observability and messaging gap. It hides wrong ends but does not cause them.
- Field: demonstrated (10 of 74 stops have no cause row). A 2, B 6, C 2. daemon.log has no row naming a stop route in the 3 s before any of them. None of the 6 early ends raised an owner-facing row either; the owner restarted one by hand (20261022-151521 after 22.9 s) and answered a fresh prompt for another (20261009-121844, 68.8 s after the chop).
- Build eras: A, B, C
- Verifier: Definition: for each coordinator state_change from recording to stopping, look back 3 s for a cause row. Result: 62 lifecycle/ended, 1 auto_stop_silence, 11 none. The era split differs from the checker's (A 2, B 6, C 2) because my A/B boundary was 2026-10-08 vs a split inside 2026-10-12; the total is about the same. Build C has both cause-less stops (15:19:54Z and 15:56:54Z on 2026-11-12), so the signature is in the newest era. The resume after an end (recording_resumed_after_end at 2026-11-12T15:46:06Z) also raised no owner-facing notice beyond the generic start. Severity lowered because the defect is the missing cause and copy, not a wrong stop.
- Direction: StartCause and StopCause on the session, logged on recording_started and recording_stopped and shown in the stop banner, HUD and Library row. A two minute post-stop watch: if presence returns, resume into the same session and badge it 'ended early, resumed'.

**D071. Before the recorder is armed one 'reliable' weak signal still ends at once: the prompt vanishes or a seconds-old recording is stopped**

- Severity low (confirmed). Location `daemon/Sources/MeetingPipeCore/Lifecycle/PromotionEngine.swift:382`. Related: START1, REC7, END14, END17, F030. Effort S.
- Owner sees: The Record panel disappears when the owner looks at another browser tab or the call window leaves the screen, then returns on the next sighting with a fresh 30 s clock.
- Mechanism: In .starting (handleStarting, PromotionEngine.swift about lines 349 to 386 at HEAD) a reliable weak end such as window-gone or a browser tab title leaving the pattern ends the engagement at once unless a live process-audio reading vetoes it. END17's hold applies only from .inMeeting. The field pack shows it mostly in build A, before END14 added the Teams process-audio veto: 315 lifecycle/ended rows whose previous phase for that bundle was starting (A 315, B 2, C 1), 307 of them within 1.0 s of starting. Leads: shareable_content_window_gone on Teams 315, browser_tab_title_left_meet_pattern on Chrome 3. At HEAD, Teams is mostly covered by the veto while it holds the mic; browsers have no vetoer.
- Field: demonstrated (308 engagements ended within 1.0 s of .starting (A 307, C 1); 316 prompts killed by an end in .starting). Build A: 306 Teams engagements and 1 Chrome ended within 1.0 s of lifecycle/starting; 306 prompts dismissed a median 0.0 s after prompt_shown, 9 more after 0.2 to 23.6 s (e.g. 2026-10-09T09:15:31Z, visible 23.6 s). Build B: 1 prompt dismissed after 16.7 s (2026-11-11T07:30:48Z) once the mic had also gone, which looks like a real end. Build C: 1, on the resume path (see D046). No coordinator/recording_ended_during_start row exists in the pack. The build A burst is END14's Teams title change (known, filed 2026-10-12); the build C case is F030's race.
- Build eras: A (2026-10-08T14:31Z to 2026-10-12T08:11Z), C once
- Verifier: Code at HEAD and at c56f2869 both have phase = .ended directly in the starting handler for a non-corroboration signal. My script counted ends from the starting phase by era (A before 2026-10-12T12Z, C from 2026-11-12T09:19Z). Build C's one case is the 2026-11-12T15:46:06.614Z end that fired right after recording_resumed_after_end (window_gone lead, confirmed_by empty), which is the resume race. Build B has 2. So the defect is real and demonstrated, but almost all of its volume is in build A, before END14. In the newest era it is rare on this Teams-heavy machine, which is why severity is lowered.
- Direction: Treat .starting like a recording for end purposes: a weak end marks the prompt 'call may have ended' and confirms on the same dwell; never emit an end from a signal that has not read live in this session.

**D077. .stopping has no exit but the stop task, and while it lasts detection is off and Record is ignored without feedback**

- Severity low (unverified low). Location `daemon/Sources/MeetingPipe/MeetingSessionController.swift:294`. Related: REC13, REC27, UX79, PERF17. Effort M.
- Owner sees: A meeting that starts while the previous recording is still being merged is not noticed until the merge returns, and pressing Record does nothing.
- Mechanism: stopRecording sets .stopping and awaits recorder.stop; only that continuation reaches resetAfterStop. The 90 s watchdog logs and notifies but changes nothing. In .stopping toggleManual breaks silently, recordAttached is refused, and at HEAD discovery does not scan. The merge took 0.6 to 2.0 s in the three logged stops; its own ceiling is minutes.
- Field: demonstrated (12 of 74 stops took over 10 s, 4 over 30 s). state_change recording->stopping to stopping->idle: median 3.6 s, p90 12.8 s, max 53.8 s (2026-10-22T07:43:13Z). No record_attached_ignored row and no call that began inside one of those windows, so the harm is not shown.
- Build eras: A 1, B 11 of the 12 over 10 s; C max 2.3 s
- Direction: Decouple finalize from the session: hand the capture files to a background finalizer and return the session machine to idle at once.

**D101. A real call drop longer than the 60 s pair dwell splits one meeting into two recordings with a hole**

- Severity low (confirmed). Location `the repo root/daemon/Sources/MeetingPipeCore/Lifecycle/PromotionEngine.swift:150`. Related: D028, audit G001, F046, ADR 0017, END17 item 5. Effort M.
- Owner sees: One meeting shows up as two; the notes of the first stop mid-topic and the second starts mid-sentence.
- Mechanism: PromotionEngine.defaultConfirmDwell = 60 is at HEAD line 150; it entered with END9 (86269d3a, 2026-08-20) and the hold, trim and resume came with END17 (01e904a5, 2026-10-24), so none of this exists at c56f2869 (the label 'both' is wrong for the home Mac). Field, era C, one occurrence: process_audio false at 15:44:27.920Z, ending_provisional and lifecycle_end_held (confirm_dwell_sec 60) immediately, Leave invalid at 15:44:29.108Z, lifecycle ended at 15:45:27.963Z with confirmed_by [ax_leave_button_invalid, shareable_content_window_gone], held_end_trimmed limit 1280.5 s, held_end_excluded, then recording_resumed_after_end at 15:46:06.606Z (since_end_sec 38.6) into 20261112-174606 whose meta carries resumed_from 20261112-172307 (the only such meta). This is the ADR 0017 resume-into-new-stem design with a Merge offer, so the cost is a split plus a 39 s unrecorded gap, not lost audio for the held 60 s.
- Field: demonstrated. process_audio false and ax_leave_button_state invalid inside a recording, lifecycle_end_held with live_signal call_is_down, lifecycle/ended 60 s later with confirmed_by [ax_leave_button_invalid, shareable_content_window_gone], held_end_trimmed at the hold start, then recording_resumed_after_end inside 120 s and a meta sidecar carrying resumed_from. FOUND once: hold 2026-11-12T15:44:27.928Z, ended 15:45:27.963Z, resumed 15:46:06.606Z; stems 20261112-172307 and 20261112-174606 (the only meta with resumed_from).
- Verifier: Code and log both match the described sequence. It is a designed trade-off documented in docs/decisions/0017 (resume window 120 s, merge restores the held tail), occurred once in the pack, and the held minute is restorable, so severity is lowered to low. The resumed engine ending at once with confirmed_by [] is a separate defect.
- Direction: Treat a same-app return inside the resume window as the same meeting: keep the recorder running through that window and stop and trim only if nothing returns, or on resume restore the held tail and join the pair automatically rather than offering a merge.

| END17 | 2026-10-24 (01e904a5) | Two tiers: only app quit, Teams' teardown and user Stop end at once; every weak end holds with the recorder running and confirms on a 60 s pair or 720 s lone dwell, never while the mic reads live; tail trimmed; 120 s promptless resume. | Converts early stops into late stops plus a trim anchored at the first weak signal, so a wrong hold now costs content instead of the recording; browser has no veto; pair and lone dwell have the stale-stamp and aged-hold gaps; native apps have no quit signal. | partly |
| END17 (folds END15) | 2026-10-24 | Two tiers: app quit, teardown and user Stop end at once; every other end holds with the recorder running and confirms on a 60 s pair or 720 s lone dwell; keep the tail and suggest a trim; resume within 120 s; retire the debounce, the browser floor, the cap and the ceiling. | Early chops for late ends everywhere except Teams, a 12 minute browser overrun by design, and a cut pinned to the first doubt (G001, F032). | partly |
| END17 / ADR 0017 | 2026-10-24 | Definitive and weak tiers, 60 s pair and 12 minute lone dwell, keep and trim the tail, resume inside 120 s | Every non-Teams end is 1 to 12 minutes late by design; the trim rides one exit (G031) and one stamp (G001, F032); resume and hold-revert record the next call without consent; the app-quit tier is not wired for native apps (F033) | partly |

| MIC15 | 2026-07-11 | Log the captured input device, warn at start on an idle default input, warn at stop on a dead mic | Warn-only; the wrong device is still recorded, and one loud buffer passes the check | partly |

| PERF6 | 2026-07-11 | Gated the AX control walks behind cheap preconditions and backed the discovery poll off from 3 s to 12 s when quiet. | Up to 12 s before a join with no workspace event is even looked at; reopened 2026-10-26 because DET5 reopened the walk gate (F038). | partly |
| PERF15, PERF17, PERF19 | 2026-10-26 | AX build and first ScreenCaptureKit read moved off main; one scan in flight and none outside idle; an unavailable read arms the poll. | Any state wedged outside idle now stops scanning altogether. | yes |
| PERF15 | 2026-10-26 | AX handle builds moved off main; record start waits at most 1.5 s for them. | Lifecycle engage and Leave arming now land after the recorder is up; under NDA the mic start can be zero-filled up to 1.5 s. | yes |
| PERF17 | 2026-10-26 | No discovery scans outside idle; a mic-busy span seen then is latched as handled | Makes explicit what the installed build already did by dropping the prompt and setting the latch: a span seen outside idle is consumed, so the catch-all cannot re-prompt for a call whose recording ended falsely | yes |
| PERF19 | 2026-10-26 | Poll backoff held in steady state (AX and process audio 5 s, window 1 s); one ScreenCaptureKit request in flight; a nil initial probe re-arms the poll. | Less idle cost for up to 5 s more end latency, which the dwells absorb. | yes |

| REC17 | 2026-09-18 | Idle backstop holds its streak while the system channel is down | None found on the mic side | yes |

## Signals the detector uses today

| Signal | API | Semantics | Latency | Role | Weaknesses |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Browser window title via Accessibility | AXUIElementCopyAttributeValue kAXWindowsAttribute then kAXTitleAttribute, per running browser process | poll | 80 ms after a trigger; otherwise up to the 3 s or 12 s backstop poll | Admission gate 1: decides whether a browser is a candidate at all | Carries only the selected tab of each window; says nothing about a background tab. Depends on a third-party page title. Requires Accessibility trust cached at launch. What it returns for a window on another Space or a full-screen Space is not readable from code (inferred to be absent). |
| On-screen window title via ScreenCaptureKit | SCShareableContent.getExcludingDesktopWindows(false, onScreenWindowsOnly: true), behind CGPreflightScreenCaptureAccess | poll | about 15 to 20 ms in the two Safari starts in the log; 1 s poll afterwards; 2 s timeout pe [...] | Admission gate 2 and the only source of the starting verdict (the prompt) for a plain browser; also the only end signal | Needs the Screen Recording grant, which the docs describe as audio-only. On-screen only, so minimised windows and other Spaces read as gone. Same selected-tab limit as the AX title. One process-wide request at a time. |
| Workspace app notifications | NSWorkspace didLaunch, didTerminate, didActivate, didDeactivate | edge | immediate plus 80 ms coalesce | Scan trigger and cadence activity marker for listed bundles | Fires on app switches only. Opening or joining a meeting in a tab of an already frontmost browser raises nothing. A scan fired by activation usually runs before the page title has loaded. |
| Mic busy | AVCaptureDevice.default(for: .audio).isInUseByAnotherApplication, KVO plus poll sample | mixed | intended immediate; in the field never | Scan trigger, DET3 mic-busy spans, and the DET1 catch-all prompt after a 30 s dwell | Zero spans in the whole event log while the mic was demonstrably held. Device resolved once at launch and never rebound. No attribution: the holder is assumed to be the frontmost app at the moment of the grab. Cause of the zero is unestablished (DET10 probe owner-owed). |
| Backstop poll | Schedulers.mainRunLoopOnce at HEAD, Timer.scheduledTimer in the installed build | poll | 3 s after meeting-app activity, 12 s once one poll passes quiet | The only path that notices a tab title change | A level fact (a call is running) is sampled at instants; the Meet tab must be selected and on screen at a sample. Skipped entirely outside idle at HEAD. |
| Per-process audio input | kAudioHardwarePropertyProcessObjectList plus kAudioProcessPropertyIsRunningInput | level | 2 ms after engage in the log (Zoom, 2026-10-13) | Native Teams and Zoom only: start corroborator at HEAD (DET12), end veto. Not read for any browser or PWA | Deliberately excluded for browsers (readsProcessAudio is native-only), so the one signal that reads true in this log when a call holds the mic is not used where the title gate is weakest. Which process object holds input for Safari or Chrome is unmeasured. |
| URL fragments | substring test of meeting_apps.toml url_fragments against the AX window title | poll | same as the AX title | Nominal admission disjunct | Browsers put the page title, not the address, in the window title, so it almost never fires (DET6 states this; the URL readability probe is owner-owed since 2026-08-18). |
| PWA identity | NSRunningApplication.localizedName plus bundle id prefix (Chrome, Edge, Brave app prefixes) | level | same scan | Admits an installed Meet or Teams web app as a browser-kind candidate | A name-only admission carries no title match and needs a corroborator; Leave and Mute matchers return nothing for a non-native bundle, so only the toolbar needle walk could supply one. Safari Add to Dock apps and other Chromium browsers are not covered. |
| Workspace termination and PWA window title observer | NSWorkspace termination, AX title-changed observer | edge | immediate | End-side only; listed for completeness | Contribute nothing to start detection. |
| Workspace app events | NSWorkspace didLaunch, didTerminate, didActivate, didDeactivate notifications | edge | 80 ms coalesce, then one scan | Scan trigger for native, browser and PWA bundles; marks the next poll fast | Fires on app switches, not on joining a call inside an app that is already frontmost; no sleep, wake or unlock event is observed |
| Mic busy (device level) | AVCaptureDevice.default(for: .audio).isInUseByAnotherApplication, KVO plus poll | mixed | never observed to fire; 30 s dwell by design | DET1 catch-all prompt for browsers and FaceTime, Discord, WhatsApp, Telegram; DET3 miss audit; intended fast scan trigger | Zero spans in every log generation read; device resolved once at launch and never rebound; names the frontmost app, not the process on the mic; excludes whitelist natives |
| AX window title | AXUIElementCopyAttributeValue kAXWindows, kAXTitle per window, matched by isActiveMeetingWindow | poll | per scan | titleMatch (weight 2); admits the control walk | Zoom: one English literal, 'zoom meeting'. Teams: accepts any non-chrome title, so it is true around the clock. Needs Accessibility |
| AX call controls | Three recursive AX walks per candidate: toolbar by title needle, Leave by label, Mute by MuteLabels catalogue | poll | per scan; up to 4000 nodes per window per walk | The corroborators that make a native confident (weights 4, 3, 2) | Present only while the app renders them: Zoom hides them, Teams compact view hides them for 21 to 48 s (task history); toolbar and Leave labels are English only; Zoom toolbar needle matched on 0 of 27 rows; costly on the meeting app's main thread |
| Process audio, start side (HEAD only) | kAudioHardwarePropertyProcessObjectList plus kAudioProcessPropertyIsRunningInput, one enumeration per scan | poll | per scan | DET12 corroborator (weight 3) for Teams and Zoom; needs no TCC grant | Not in the installed build; a level fact sampled by poll instead of listened to; alone it never clears the bar; Zoom never measured, Webex and Slack excluded; not used to keep the skip latch alive |
| Process audio, lifecycle | ProcessAudioSignal over CoreAudioHALBus: enumerate the bundle's HAL process objects and read IsRunningInput | mixed | immediate at engage (true at all six Zoom engages in the log) | First live primary, so it is what turns an engage into starting; at HEAD vetoes weak ends | Engaged only after a confident scan; first reading is forwarded as an edge (F030); a vanished object freezes the last value (F033) |
| ScreenCaptureKit window presence | SCShareableContent.getExcludingDesktopWindows(false, onScreenWindowsOnly: true), 1 s poll at HEAD | poll | 1 s | Lifecycle primary: live can raise starting, gone leads an end | Off-screen is reported as gone (other Space, minimised, full-screen change): the 16 s recording; needs Screen Recording |
| AX Leave button liveness | AXObserver AXUIElementDestroyed on the resolved Leave element, with a re-resolve walk | mixed | immediate edge | Lifecycle primary; healthy can raise starting, invalid leads an end (needs corroboration) | Destroyed whenever the toolbar hides (seen 11 s after a prompt at 08:09:00Z); same evidence class as window presence |
| Suppression clocks | RepromptCooldown (60 s from an end, skip or timeout), SkippedMeetingLatch (60 s past the last confident sighting), ResumeAfterEnd (120 s, HEAD), prompt timeout [...] | level | wall clock | Decide whether a confident sighting prompts, stays silent or records without asking | Keyed by bundle id and time, in memory only; no meeting identity; a suppressed sighting logs nothing on the latch path |
| ProcessAudioSignal (process_audio_is_running_input) | kAudioHardwarePropertyProcessObjectList enumeration matched by bundle prefix or PID ancestry, then kAudioProcessPropertyIsRunningInput OR-ed across objects; HAL [...] | edge | Listener immediate when delivered; poll 1 s backing off to 5 s; an unreadable set holds th [...] | The only call-state reading. Since END14 and END17 its live value vetoes window-gone ends and both dwells; its false is a weak end that can lead or pair. Teams and Zoom only; never [...] | Emits on change only, so the engine cannot tell 'still live' from 'last heard live an hour ago'; a vanished object reads nil and freezes the veto at live; the first reading is emitted even when false; object churn re-resolves on every system audio client change; Zoom mute behaviour unmeasured; says nothing for listen-only joins |
